a cookie stringcreate main schema
                -- Drop existing tables if they exist
                DROP TABLE IF EXISTS session_messages;
                DROP TABLE IF EXISTS todo_lists;
                DROP TABLE IF EXISTS instance_processes;
                DROP TABLE IF EXISTS notes;
                DROP TABLE IF EXISTS sessions;
                DROP TABLE IF EXISTS repositories;
                -- Create repos table
                CREATE TABLE repos (
                    id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                    remote_url TEXT,
                    name TEXT,
                    default_branch TEXT DEFAULT 'main',
                    root_path TEXT,
                    setup_script TEXT,
                    created_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT (datetime('now')),
                    updated_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT (datetime('now'))
                -- Create workspaces table
                CREATE TABLE workspaces (
                    id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                    repository_id TEXT,
                    city_name TEXT,
                    directory_name TEXT,
                    archived INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
                    active_session_id TEXT,
                    branch TEXT,
                    created_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT (datetime('now')),
                    updated_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT (datetime('now'))
                -- Create sessions table
                CREATE TABLE sessions (
                    id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                    status TEXT DEFAULT 'idle',
                    claude_session_id TEXT,
                    created_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT (datetime('now')),
                    updated_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT (datetime('now'))
                -- Create session_messages table
                CREATE TABLE session_messages (
                    id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                    session_id TEXT,
                    role TEXT,
                    content TEXT,
                    created_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT (datetime('now'))
                -- Create update triggers for repos
                CREATE TRIGGER update_repos_updated_at
                AFTER UPDATE ON repos
                BEGIN
                    UPDATE repos SET updated_at = datetime('now')
                    WHERE id = NEW.id;
                END;
                -- Create update triggers for workspaces
                CREATE TRIGGER update_workspaces_updated_at
                AFTER UPDATE ON workspaces
                BEGIN
                    UPDATE workspaces SET updated_at = datetime('now')
                    WHERE id = NEW.id;
                END;
                -- Create update triggers for sessions
                CREATE TRIGGER update_sessions_updated_at
                AFTER UPDATE ON sessions
                BEGIN
                    UPDATE sessions SET updated_at = datetime('now')
                    WHERE id = NEW.id;
                END;
            create settings table
                -- Create settings table
                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS settings (
                    key TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                    value TEXT NOT NULL,
                    created_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT (datetime('now')),
                    updated_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT (datetime('now'))
                -- Create update trigger for settings
                CREATE TRIGGER update_settings_updated_at
                AFTER UPDATE ON settings
                BEGIN
                    UPDATE settings SET updated_at = datetime('now')
                    WHERE key = NEW.key;
                END;
                -- Insert default settings
                INSERT OR IGNORE INTO settings (key, value) VALUES ('claude_model', 'sonnet');
            add unread_count to sessions table
                -- Add unread_count column to sessions table
                ALTER TABLE sessions ADD COLUMN unread_count INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
            add pending_message to sessions table
                -- Add pending_message column to sessions table
                -- This is a workaround for the "Potato Problem": Claude Code crashes with exit code 1
                -- if cancelled before acknowledging the first user message. We store pending messages
                -- to prevent loss and disable cancellation until acknowledged.
                -- TODO: Remove this column once Claude Code properly handles early cancellation.
                ALTER TABLE sessions ADD COLUMN pending_message TEXT;
            add columns for compaction tracking to sessions table
                    ALTER TABLE sessions ADD COLUMN freshly_compacted INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
                    ALTER TABLE sessions ADD COLUMN context_token_count INTEGER DEFAULT 0;                    
                add notes column to sessions table
                    ALTER TABLE sessions ADD COLUMN notes TEXT;
                drop pending_message column from sessions table
                    -- SQLite doesn't support DROP COLUMN directly before version 3.35.0 (2021)
                    -- So we need to recreate the table without the column
                    
                    -- Create new table without pending_message column
                    CREATE TABLE sessions_new (
                        id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                        status TEXT DEFAULT 'idle',
                        claude_session_id TEXT,
                        unread_count INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
                        freshly_compacted INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
                        context_token_count INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
                        notes TEXT,
                        created_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT (datetime('now')),
                        updated_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT (datetime('now'))
                    );
                    
                    -- Copy data from old table to new table (excluding pending_message)
                    INSERT INTO sessions_new (id, status, claude_session_id, unread_count, freshly_compacted, context_token_count, notes, created_at, updated_at)
                    SELECT id, status, claude_session_id, unread_count, freshly_compacted, context_token_count, notes, created_at, updated_at
                    FROM sessions;
                    
                    -- Drop old table
                    DROP TABLE sessions;
                    
                    -- Rename new table to sessions
                    ALTER TABLE sessions_new RENAME TO sessions;
                    
                    -- Recreate the update trigger
                    CREATE TRIGGER update_sessions_updated_at
                    AFTER UPDATE ON sessions
                    BEGIN
                        UPDATE sessions SET updated_at = datetime('now')
                        WHERE id = NEW.id;
                    END;
                create attachments table
                -- Create attachments table
                CREATE TABLE attachments (
                    id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                    type TEXT,
                    original_name TEXT,
                    path TEXT,
                    is_loading INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
                    session_id TEXT,
                    session_message_id TEXT,
                    is_draft INTEGER DEFAULT 1,
                    created_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT (datetime('now'))
                -- Create indexes for efficient queries
                CREATE INDEX idx_attachments_session_id ON attachments(session_id);
                CREATE INDEX idx_attachments_session_message_id ON attachments(session_message_id);
                CREATE INDEX idx_attachments_is_draft ON attachments(is_draft);
            add sent_at for message queueing
                -- Add sent_at column to session_messages table
                -- When NULL, indicates the message is queued and waiting to be sent to Claude
                -- When NOT NULL, indicates the message has been sent to Claude
                -- For non-queued messages, this will be the same as created_at
                ALTER TABLE session_messages ADD COLUMN sent_at TEXT;
                -- Backfill sent_at with created_at for existing messages
                UPDATE session_messages SET sent_at = created_at WHERE sent_at IS NULL;
                -- Create index for efficient sorting
                CREATE INDEX idx_session_messages_sent_at ON session_messages(session_id, sent_at);
            add full_message column for slash commands
                -- Add full_message column to session_messages table
                -- This stores the processed message content that was actually sent to Claude
                -- For slash commands, this contains the expanded command content
                -- For regular messages, this is NULL
                ALTER TABLE session_messages ADD COLUMN full_message TEXT;
            add is_compacting column to track compaction state
                -- Add is_compacting column to sessions table
                -- This indicates whether a session is currently being compacted
                -- When 1, messages are queued instead of sent immediately
                -- When 0 (default), messages are sent normally
                ALTER TABLE sessions ADD COLUMN is_compacting INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
            add model column to sessions table for per-chat model selection
                -- Add model column to sessions table
                -- This allows each chat to have its own model selection
                -- If NULL, uses the global default from settings
                ALTER TABLE sessions ADD COLUMN model TEXT;
            update default model to opus
                -- Update the default model to opus for all users
                UPDATE settings SET value = 'opus' WHERE key = 'claude_model';
            add cancelled_at column to track cancelled messages
                -- Add cancelled_at column to session_messages table
                -- This stores the timestamp when a message was cancelled
                -- When NULL, the message was not cancelled
                -- When set, the message and all subsequent queued messages were cancelled
                ALTER TABLE session_messages ADD COLUMN cancelled_at TEXT;
                -- Create index for efficient queries on cancelled messages
                CREATE INDEX idx_session_messages_cancelled_at ON session_messages(session_id, cancelled_at);
            add model column to session_messages for per-message model selection
                -- Add model column to session_messages table
                -- This stores the model that should be used for processing this message
                -- If NULL, uses the session's model or global default
                ALTER TABLE session_messages ADD COLUMN model TEXT;
            add storage_version and archive_script columns
                -- Add storage_version column to repos table
                -- Version 1: Old storage structure (/<repo>/.main, /<repo>/<workspace>)
                -- Version 2: New storage structure (<rootpath>, <rootpath>/conductor/<workspace>)
                -- Default to 1 for backwards compatibility with existing repos
                ALTER TABLE repos ADD COLUMN storage_version INTEGER DEFAULT 1;
                -- Add archive_script column to repos table  
                -- This script runs in the linked workspace when archiving it
                ALTER TABLE repos ADD COLUMN archive_script TEXT;
            add unread column to workspaces table
                -- Add unread column to workspaces table
                -- This indicates whether a workspace has unread activity
                -- When 1, the workspace should be marked visually as unread
                -- When 0 (default), the workspace has been viewed
                ALTER TABLE workspaces ADD COLUMN unread INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
            add display_order column to repos table
                -- Add display_order column to repos table
                -- This allows users to reorder repositories via drag and drop
                -- Defaults to 0, which will be updated with proper values
                ALTER TABLE repos ADD COLUMN display_order INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
                -- Update existing repos to have sequential display_order based on created_at
                -- This ensures existing repos maintain their current order
                UPDATE repos
                SET display_order = (
                    SELECT COUNT(*) 
                    FROM repos r2 
                    WHERE r2.created_at < repos.created_at 
                       OR (r2.created_at = repos.created_at AND r2.id < repos.id)
            add placeholder_branch_name column to workspaces table
                -- Add placeholder_branch_name column to workspaces table
                -- This stores the original branch name when the workspace is created
                -- Used to determine if the branch rename instruction should be shown
                ALTER TABLE workspaces ADD COLUMN placeholder_branch_name TEXT;
            migrate openai provider users back to default provider
                -- Migrate users from removed OpenAI provider back to default Anthropic provider
                -- Update the provider setting from 'openai' to 'default'
                UPDATE settings 
                SET value = 'default', 
                    updated_at = datetime('now')
                WHERE key = 'claude_provider' AND value = 'openai';
                -- Ensure claude_model is set to 'opus' for affected users
                -- Only insert if it doesn't exist (users who had OpenAI might not have this setting)
                INSERT OR IGNORE INTO settings (key, value)
                VALUES ('claude_model', 'opus');
            add state column to workspaces table for lifecycle management
                -- Add state column to workspaces table
                -- States: 'initializing' (workspace created but git worktree not yet created),
                --         'active' (fully functional workspace),
                --         'archived' (archived workspace)
                ALTER TABLE workspaces ADD COLUMN state TEXT DEFAULT 'active';
                -- Migrate existing archived workspaces to use the new state column
                -- Set state based on current archived boolean value
                UPDATE workspaces 
                SET state = CASE 
                    WHEN archived = 1 THEN 'archived' 
                    ELSE 'active' 
                END;
                -- Rename archived column to indicate it's deprecated
                ALTER TABLE workspaces RENAME COLUMN archived TO DEPRECATED_archived;
            add permission_mode column to sessions table
                -- Add permission_mode column to sessions table
                -- This stores the permission mode for each session (default or plan)
                -- Defaults to 'default' for backward compatibility
                ALTER TABLE sessions ADD COLUMN permission_mode TEXT DEFAULT 'default';
            deprecate cityName column in favor of directoryName
                -- Rename city_name column to indicate it's deprecated
                ALTER TABLE workspaces RENAME COLUMN city_name TO DEPRECATED_city_name;
                -- Backfill directory_name with city_name
                -- This should only apply to archived workspaces
                UPDATE workspaces
                SET directory_name = REPLACE(LOWER(DEPRECATED_city_name), ' ', '-')
                WHERE directory_name IS NULL;
            add run_script column to repos table
                -- Add run_script column to repos table
                -- This script runs when the user clicks the "Run server" button
                -- If NULL, a default script is used
                ALTER TABLE repos ADD COLUMN run_script TEXT;
            add run script configuration columns to repos table
                -- Add run_script_mode column to repos table
                -- 'concurrent': Multiple run scripts can run in parallel across workspaces
                -- 'nonconcurrent': Only one run script runs at a time
                ALTER TABLE repos ADD COLUMN run_script_mode TEXT DEFAULT 'concurrent';
            add parent_branch column to workspaces table
                -- Add parent_branch column to workspaces table
                -- This stores the branch that this workspace was created from
                -- Previously we always used the repo's default_branch, but now each workspace
                -- can have its own parent branch
                ALTER TABLE workspaces ADD COLUMN parent_branch TEXT;
                -- Backfill existing workspaces with their repo's default_branch
                -- This preserves existing behavior for all current workspaces
                UPDATE workspaces 
                SET parent_branch = (
                    SELECT default_branch 
                    FROM repos 
                    WHERE repos.id = workspaces.repository_id
                WHERE parent_branch IS NULL;
            add remote column to repos table
                -- Add remote column to repos table
                -- This stores the git remote name that should be used for operations
                -- Previously we always used 'origin', but now each repo can configure its remote
                ALTER TABLE repos ADD COLUMN remote TEXT;
                -- Backfill existing repositories with 'origin' as the default remote
                -- This preserves existing behavior for all current repositories
                UPDATE repos 
                SET remote = 'origin'
                WHERE remote IS NULL;
            add thinking_level column to sessions table
                -- Add thinking_level column to sessions table
                -- This stores the thinking level for each session (NONE, SHORT, MEDIUM, LONG)
                -- Defaults to 'NONE' for backward compatibility
                ALTER TABLE sessions ADD COLUMN thinking_level TEXT DEFAULT 'NONE';
            add big_terminal_mode column to workspaces table for per-workspace big terminal setting
                -- Add big_terminal_mode column to workspaces table
                -- This allows each workspace to have its own big terminal setting
                -- When 1, shows terminal as main pane instead of Claude Code interface
                -- When 0 (default), shows normal layout
                ALTER TABLE workspaces ADD COLUMN big_terminal_mode INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
            update workspace states to 5-state system
                -- Update workspace states to new 5-state system
                -- States: 'initializing', 'setting_up', 'ready', 'archiving', 'archived'
                -- Migrate all 'active' workspaces to 'ready'
                UPDATE workspaces 
                SET state = 'ready'
                WHERE state = 'active';
                -- Note: 'initializing' and 'archived' states remain unchanged
                -- New states 'setting_up' and 'archiving' will be used for new operations
            add setup_log_path column to workspaces table
                -- Add setup_log_path column to workspaces table
                -- This stores the path to the setup script log file for debugging
                ALTER TABLE workspaces ADD COLUMN setup_log_path TEXT;
            add initialization_log_path column to workspaces table
                -- Add initialization_log_path column to workspaces table
                -- This stores the path to the git worktree initialization log file for debugging
                ALTER TABLE workspaces ADD COLUMN initialization_log_path TEXT;
            add initialization_files_copied column to workspaces table
                -- Add initialization_files_copied column to workspaces table
                -- This stores the number of files copied over during initialization
                ALTER TABLE workspaces ADD COLUMN initialization_files_copied INTEGER;
            update default model to sonnet
                -- Update the default model to sonnet for all users
                UPDATE settings SET value = 'sonnet' WHERE key = 'claude_model';
            add last_user_message_at column to sessions table
                -- Add last_user_message_at column to sessions table
                -- This stores the timestamp of the last user message sent in the session
                -- Used for sorting chats by last activity
                ALTER TABLE sessions ADD COLUMN last_user_message_at TEXT;
                -- Populate with existing data
                UPDATE sessions
                SET last_user_message_at = (
                    SELECT sent_at
                    FROM session_messages
                    WHERE session_messages.session_id = sessions.id
                    AND session_messages.role = 'user'
                    AND session_messages.sent_at IS NOT NULL
                    ORDER BY session_messages.sent_at DESC
                    LIMIT 1
            reset permission_mode to default for all sessions
                -- Reset permission_mode to default for all existing sessions
                UPDATE sessions
                SET permission_mode = 'default'
                WHERE permission_mode != 'default';
            add columns for checkpointing 
                -- Add sdk_message_id and last_assistant_message_id columns to session_messages table
                ALTER TABLE session_messages ADD COLUMN sdk_message_id TEXT;
                -- Add resume_session_at column to track where to resume Claude session after revert
                ALTER TABLE sessions ADD COLUMN resume_session_at TEXT;
                -- Add last_assistant_message_id columns to session_messages table
                ALTER TABLE session_messages ADD COLUMN last_assistant_message_id TEXT;
            add workspace_id to sessions for multiple sessions per workspace
                -- Add workspace_id column to sessions table
                -- This enables one-to-many relationship: workspace has many sessions
                ALTER TABLE sessions ADD COLUMN workspace_id TEXT;
                -- Backfill workspace_id from existing active_session_id relationships
                -- For each session, find the workspace that has it as active_session_id
                UPDATE sessions
                SET workspace_id = (
                    SELECT id FROM workspaces WHERE active_session_id = sessions.id
                WHERE workspace_id IS NULL;
                -- Create index for efficient queries
                CREATE INDEX idx_sessions_workspace_id ON sessions(workspace_id);
            add is_hidden column to sessions table
                -- Add is_hidden column to sessions table
                -- This allows soft-deleting sessions by hiding them instead of deleting
                -- When 1, the session is hidden from the UI
                -- When 0 (default), the session is visible
                ALTER TABLE sessions ADD COLUMN is_hidden INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
            add agent_type column to sessions table
                -- Add agent_type column to sessions table
                -- This stores which agent backend created the session
                ALTER TABLE sessions ADD COLUMN agent_type;
                -- Ensure existing sessions default to Claude
                UPDATE sessions
                SET agent_type = 'claude'
                WHERE agent_type IS NULL;
            rename claude_model setting to default_model
                -- Rename claude_model setting key to default_model
                -- This makes the setting name more generic since it can now store Codex models
                UPDATE settings
                SET key = 'default_model'
                WHERE key = 'claude_model';
            migrate automatic and opusplan models to sonnet
                -- Migrate users from removed "automatic" and "opusplan" models to "sonnet"
                -- These models were deprecated and should fall back to the default "sonnet" model
                -- Update default_model setting
                UPDATE settings
                SET value = 'sonnet'
                WHERE key = 'default_model' AND (value = 'automatic' OR value = 'opusplan');
                -- Update session-level model overrides
                UPDATE sessions
                SET model = 'sonnet'
                WHERE model = 'automatic' OR model = 'opusplan';
            clear checkpointing data for pre-release messages
                -- Clear last_assistant_message_id for all existing messages
                -- This disables git checkpointing/revert functionality for messages created before
                -- the checkpointing feature was officially released to users
                -- New messages after this migration will continue to have checkpointing enabled
                UPDATE session_messages SET last_assistant_message_id = NULL;
            add turn_id column to session_messages and backfill turn data
                -- Add turn_id column to session_messages for turn membership
                ALTER TABLE session_messages ADD COLUMN turn_id TEXT;
                -- Index turn_id for fast lookup
                CREATE INDEX idx_session_messages_turn_id
                ON session_messages(turn_id);
                -- User messages become their own turn leaders
                UPDATE session_messages
                SET turn_id = id
                WHERE role = 'user'
                  AND (sent_at IS NOT NULL OR cancelled_at IS NOT NULL);
                -- Assistant messages join the most recent user turn in the same session
                UPDATE session_messages
                SET turn_id = (
                    SELECT id
                    FROM session_messages um
                    WHERE um.session_id = session_messages.session_id
                      AND um.role = 'user'
                      AND (um.sent_at IS NOT NULL OR um.cancelled_at IS NOT NULL)
                      AND COALESCE(um.sent_at, um.cancelled_at) <= session_messages.sent_at
                    ORDER BY COALESCE(um.sent_at, um.cancelled_at) DESC
                    LIMIT 1
                WHERE role IN ('assistant', 'SUMMARY')
                  AND sent_at IS NOT NULL;
            add title column to sessions table
                -- Add non-nullable title column to sessions table
                ALTER TABLE sessions ADD COLUMN title TEXT DEFAULT 'Untitled';
                -- Backfill title with existing agent type, mapping "codex" to "Codex" and "claude" to "Claude"
                UPDATE sessions
                SET title = 'Codex'
                WHERE agent_type = 'codex';
                UPDATE sessions
                SET title = 'Claude'
                WHERE agent_type = 'claude';
            migrate provider settings to claude_env_vars
                -- Migrate old provider-specific settings into claude_env_vars
                WITH provider_settings AS (
                    SELECT
                        MAX(CASE WHEN key = 'anthropic_base_url' THEN value END) as anthropic_base_url,
                        MAX(CASE WHEN key = 'anthropic_auth_token' THEN value END) as anthropic_auth_token,
                        MAX(CASE WHEN key = 'anthropic_api_key' THEN value END) as anthropic_api_key,
                        MAX(CASE WHEN key = 'http_proxy' THEN value END) as http_proxy,
                        MAX(CASE WHEN key = 'bedrock_profile' THEN value END) as bedrock_profile,
                        MAX(CASE WHEN key = 'vertex_project_id' THEN value END) as vertex_project_id
                    FROM settings
                    WHERE key IN ('anthropic_base_url', 'anthropic_auth_token', 'anthropic_api_key',
                                  'http_proxy', 'bedrock_profile', 'vertex_project_id')
                INSERT OR REPLACE INTO settings (key, value, created_at, updated_at)
                SELECT
                    'claude_env_vars',
                    LTRIM(
                        CASE WHEN anthropic_base_url IS NOT NULL THEN 'ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL=' || anthropic_base_url ELSE '' END ||
                        CASE WHEN anthropic_auth_token IS NOT NULL THEN CHAR(10) || 'ANTHROPIC_AUTH_TOKEN=' || anthropic_auth_token ELSE '' END ||
                        CASE WHEN anthropic_api_key IS NOT NULL THEN CHAR(10) || 'ANTHROPIC_API_KEY=' || anthropic_api_key ELSE '' END ||
                        CASE WHEN http_proxy IS NOT NULL THEN
                            CHAR(10) || 'HTTP_PROXY=' || http_proxy ||
                            CHAR(10) || 'HTTPS_PROXY=' || http_proxy
                        ELSE '' END ||
                        CASE WHEN bedrock_profile IS NOT NULL THEN
                            CHAR(10) || 'CLAUDE_CODE_USE_BEDROCK=1' ||
                            CHAR(10) || 'AWS_PROFILE=' || bedrock_profile
                        ELSE '' END ||
                        CASE WHEN vertex_project_id IS NOT NULL THEN
                            CHAR(10) || 'CLAUDE_CODE_USE_VERTEX=true' ||
                            CHAR(10) || 'ANTHROPIC_VERTEX_PROJECT_ID=' || vertex_project_id
                        ELSE '' END,
                        CHAR(10) || ' '
                    ),
                    datetime('now'),
                    datetime('now')
                FROM provider_settings
                WHERE anthropic_base_url IS NOT NULL
                   OR anthropic_auth_token IS NOT NULL
                   OR anthropic_api_key IS NOT NULL
                   OR http_proxy IS NOT NULL
                   OR bedrock_profile IS NOT NULL

  const osName = __TEMPLATE_os_name__
  //-----------------------//
  // drag on mousedown and maximize on double click on Windows and Linux
  // while macOS macos maximization should be on mouseup and if the mouse
  // moves after the double click, it should be cancelled (see https://github.com/tauri-apps/tauri/issues/8306)
  //-----------------------//
  const TAURI_DRAG_REGION_ATTR = 'data-tauri-drag-region'
  // initial mousedown position for macOS
  document.addEventListener('mousedown', (e) => {
    const attr = e.target.getAttribute(TAURI_DRAG_REGION_ATTR)
      // element has the magic data attribute
      //  and not false
      && attr !== 'false'
      // and was left mouse button
      && e.button === 0
      // and was normal click to drag or double click to maximize
      && (e.detail === 1 || e.detail === 2)
      // macOS maximization happens on `mouseup`,
      // so we save needed state and early return
      if (osName === 'macos' && e.detail == 2) {
        initialX = e.clientX
        initialY = e.clientY
      // prevents text cursor
      e.preventDefault()
      // fix #2549: double click on drag region edge causes content to maximize without window sizing change
      // https://github.com/tauri-apps/tauri/issues/2549#issuecomment-1250036908
      e.stopImmediatePropagation()
      // start dragging if the element has a `tauri-drag-region` data attribute and maximize on double-clicking it
      const cmd = e.detail === 2 ? 'internal_toggle_maximize' : 'start_dragging'
      window.__TAURI_INTERNALS__.invoke('plugin:window|' + cmd)
  // on macOS we maximze on mouseup instead, to match the system behavior where maximization can be canceled
  // if the mouse moves outside the data-tauri-drag-region
  if (osName === 'macos') {
    document.addEventListener('mouseup', (e) => {
      const attr = e.target.getAttribute(TAURI_DRAG_REGION_ATTR)
        // element has the magic data attribute
        attr !== null
        // and not false
        && attr !== 'false'
        // and was left mouse button
        && e.button === 0
        // and was double click
        && e.detail === 2
        // and the cursor hasn't moved from initial mousedown
        && e.clientX === initialX
        && e.clientY === initialY
        window.__TAURI_INTERNALS__.invoke(
          'plugin:window|internal_toggle_maximize'

// SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0

   * A runtime generated key to ensure an IPC call comes from an initialized frame.
   * This is declared outside the `window.__TAURI_INVOKE__` definition to prevent
   * the key from being leaked by `window.__TAURI_INVOKE__.toString()`.
  const __TAURI_INVOKE_KEY__ = __TEMPLATE_invoke_key__
  const processIpcMessage = __RAW_process_ipc_message_fn__
  const osName = __TEMPLATE_os_name__
  const fetchChannelDataCommand = __TEMPLATE_fetch_channel_data_command__
  let customProtocolIpcFailed = false
  // on Android we never use it because Android does not have support to reading the request body
  const canUseCustomProtocol = osName !== 'android'
  function sendIpcMessage(message) {
    const { cmd, callback, error, payload, options } = message
      !customProtocolIpcFailed
      && (canUseCustomProtocol || cmd === fetchChannelDataCommand)
      const { contentType, data } = processIpcMessage(payload)
      const headers = new Headers((options && options.headers) || {})
      headers.set('Content-Type', contentType)
      headers.set('Tauri-Callback', callback)
      headers.set('Tauri-Error', error)
      headers.set('Tauri-Invoke-Key', __TAURI_INVOKE_KEY__)
      fetch(window.__TAURI_INTERNALS__.convertFileSrc(cmd, 'ipc'), {
        method: 'POST',
        .then((response) => {
          const callbackId =
            response.headers.get('Tauri-Response') === 'ok' ? callback : error
          // we need to split here because on Android the content-type gets duplicated
          switch ((response.headers.get('content-type') || '').split(',')[0]) {
            case 'application/json':
              return response.json().then((r) => [callbackId, r])
            case 'text/plain':
              return response.text().then((r) => [callbackId, r])
            default:
              return response.arrayBuffer().then((r) => [callbackId, r])
        .catch((e) => {
          console.warn(
            'IPC custom protocol failed, Tauri will now use the postMessage interface instead',
          // failed to use the custom protocol IPC (either the webview blocked a custom protocol or it was a CSP error)
          // so we need to fallback to the postMessage interface
          customProtocolIpcFailed = true
          sendIpcMessage(message)
        .then(([callbackId, data]) => {
          window.__TAURI_INTERNALS__.runCallback(callbackId, data)
      // otherwise use the postMessage interface
      const { data } = processIpcMessage({
          ...options,
          customProtocolIpcBlocked: customProtocolIpcFailed
        __TAURI_INVOKE_KEY__
      window.ipc.postMessage(data)
  Object.defineProperty(window.__TAURI_INTERNALS__, 'postMessage', {
    value: sendIpcMessage
